def _convolve_fft(a,b):

Sanitize:
```
def _sanitize(p):
  p=np.asarray(p,float)
  p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0)
  p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
  s=p.sum()
  if s<=0: return None
  return p/s
```
Need ensure list not bool. Output okay.

def _grad_from_weights(w,p):

def _grad_front(w,p):
  n=len(p); M=len(w)
  L=_next_pow2(n+M-1)
  fp=fftp(p,L); fw=fftp(w[:M][::-1],L)
  out=irfft(fp*fw,L)
  start=M-1; stop=M-n-1
  h=out[start:stop:-1]  # length n
  return h
```

Soft weights:
```
def _softmax_weights(values, beta):
  v=np.asarray(values,float)
  if beta <= 1e-8: return full(1/len)
  shift=max(v)
  e=np.exp(beta*(v-shift))
  s=e.sum()
  if s==0: uniform
  return e/s
```
But values c for full conv sum1, max M ~0.75/n ~1e-4 for n=4096. beta*M variation? We should use scaled values: z = c / M (or beta_inv?); active if c close M. Use `w=softmax(beta*(c - M))` would underflow for z-M ~ -1e-4; beta up to thousands gives e^{-0.1} only. Need scale: `w=softmax(beta*(c/M - 1))`. Then differences relative. If M=1/n ~2.5e-4, c/M diff maybe. Use beta from 10 to 10000.

But softmax over length 2n-1. If many values near max, active set broad. For M active, use beta hard. ```
def _conv_self(p):
  n=len(p)
  if n*n <= 1000000: return np.convolve(p,p)
  L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
  fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
  out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
  return out[:2*n-1]
```
Use for score and gradients.

s=p.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: return None
return p/s
```

def _active_weights(c, beta):
  c=np.asarray(c,float)
  N=c.size
  if N==0: return empty
  M=max(c)
  if M<=0: return uniform
  z=c/M - 1.0
  if beta <= 1e-8: return uniform
  e=np.exp(beta*z)
  s=e.sum()
  if s <=0 or not finite: return uniform
  return e/s
```

def _mmd_search(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=80.0, beta_end=5000.0, max_evals_per_step=8):
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0: return p, _score_norm(p) if p is not None else inf

    last_alpha = alpha

    stall = 0
    best_beta = beta

       h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
       maxh = float(np.max(h))

           # random log perturbation

           for a in tests:
              logq = logp_base - a*h
              # cap underflow: maxlog = max(logq)
              q = np.exp(logq - maxlog); s=q.sum(); if s<=0 continue; q/=s
              sc = _score_fast(q,n)
              if sc < chosen_score - 1e-12:
                  chosen=q; chosen_score=sc; chosen_a=a
           if chosen is not None:
              p=chosen; cur_score=chosen_score; alpha=chosen_a
              if cur_score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
              stall=0
              # anneal beta: if improved and it large, beta*1.03; if stuck, lower?
           else:
              alpha *= 0.6; stall+=1
              if stall == 10: beta = max(beta_start*0.3, beta*0.8)
              if stall >= 30: perturb
       it +=1
       # beta schedule: every 20 iterations beta*=1.05 if no recent perturbation, up to max
       if it % 20 == 0:
           beta = min(beta_max, beta*1.05)
       if cur_score > best_score*1.2 and (it % 50 == 0):
           p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    return best, best_score
```

    else:
       stagnant? perturb slightly

```
def _random_start(n, rng, mode):
   t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   if mode==0:
       z=np.zeros(n)
       K=10
       for k in range(1,K+1):
           a=float(rng.normal(0, 1.0/(k+1)))
           b=float(rng.normal(0, 1.0/(k+1)))
           ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
           z += (a*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t+ph)+b*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*t+ph))
       p=np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z,-30,30)))
   elif mode==1: # beta powers
       a=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,4.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,4.0))
       tt=np.clip(t,1e-6,1-1e-6)
       p=np.power(tt,a)*np.power(1-tt,b)
   elif mode==2: # Gaussian bumps
       K=int(rng.integers(1,5)); p=zeros
       centers=sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K)); sigmas=rng.uniform(0.02,0.3,K); weights=rng.uniform(0.3,2,K)
       for ... p += w*exp(-((t-c)/s)**2)
   elif mode==3: # random blocks
       K=int(rng.integers(2,21)); edges=sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); blocks=[0]+edges+[1]
       heights=abs(rng.normal(0,1,K))+0.1? or lognormal
       p=zeros; for each block set height
       p=np.convolve(p, gauss small) # smooth
   elif mode==4: # smoothed exp Fourier
       z = rng.normal(0,1,n); z = np.convolve(z, gaussian sigma ~3) ; p=exp(clip(z))
   else: # logistic mix
       c=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); k=rng.uniform(3,20)
       p=1/(1+exp(-k*(t-c))) * exp(-((t-c)/0.25)^2) + 1e-6
   p=np.where(finite,p,0); p=np.maximum(p+1e-10,0); normalize
```
Need ensure p positive or zero; exp positive. For beta negative powers, may inf; clip finite, p+1e-10.

Implement `_lp_improve(p0, iters=3, lp_n=None)`:
```
def _lp_improve(p0, max_iters=5, lp_n=256):
   p=_norm_seq(p0)
   if p is None: return p
   if p.size > lp_n: p_work = _resample(p, lp_n) else p_work=p.copy()
   p_work=np.maximum(p_work, 1e-9/p_work.size); normalize
   best=p_work; best_score=...
   for _ in range(max_iters):
       g = _solve_lp_g(p_work)
       if g is None: break
       blend = line search
       if blend_score < best_score - 1e-10:
           p_work=blend; best_score=...
       else: break
   return best
```

Perturbation function:
```
def _log_perturb(p, rng, std):
   logp=np.log(p)
   logp += rng.normal(0,std,p.size)
   q=np.exp(logp - max(logp)); q/=q.sum(); return q
```

```
def _lp_solve_g(p):
    p=_norm_seq(p)
    if p is None or p.size > 512: p=_resample(p, 512) maybe
    n=p.size
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-8/float(n)); p/=p.sum()
    c=_conv_self(p); M=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite or M<=0: return None
    B=np.zeros((2*n-1,n),float)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k); cnt=hi-lo+1
       B[k,lo:hi+1]=p[k-np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
    obj=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.full(2*n-1, M)
    try:
       res=linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n)
       except: return None
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       g=np.where(np.isfinite(g)&(g>0),g,0)
       if g.sum()>0: return g/g.sum()
    # if failure, maybe partial res? if res.x and sum>0? Could use.
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
       g=...
       if g.sum()>0: return g/g.sum()
    return None
```

`_norm_seq`: Use float64.
```
def _norm_seq(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size ==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    return arr/s
```

`_score_norm` as above. For score from list, if length small maybe sum<0.01? We normalize sum=1 so official won't reject. If we choose output array with sum=1 but many zeros, okay.

`_conv_self`:
```
def _conv_self(p):
    n=p.size
    if n ==0: return np.array([],float)
    if n <= 1: return np.array([p[0]*p[0]], float)
    if n*n <= 2000000:
       return np.asarray(np.convolve(p,p), float)
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
    return out[:2*n-1].astype(float,copy=False)
```
For n=1 score=2. okay.

Potential issue: For direct np.convolve with n=1414, n*n~2M, okay. For n=2048, uses FFT. Good.

`_score_fast`:
```
def _score_fast(p,n):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size != n: n=p.size
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf
    if abs(s-1.0) > 1e-12:
        p=p/s
    c=_conv_self(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
    if m < 0: m=0
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
This normalizes each line search if s error. Good.

`_softmax_weights`:
```
def _active_weights(c, beta):
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   n=c.size
   if n==0: return np.array([],float)
   m=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(m) or m <=0:
      return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   z=c/m -1.0
   if beta <=1e-8:
      return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   e=np.exp(beta*z)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
      return np.full(n,1.0/n)
   return e/s
```
Because z max0, min -1; beta high. If c has NaN, replace: `c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)` before.

`_grad_from_weights` as above. Add direct fallback for small n*M? Maybe not necessary. But for n small, FFT overhead okay. We'll use FFT always except n=0.
```
def _grad_from_weights(w,p):
   n=p.size; M=w.size
   if n==0: return np.array([],float)
   if M==0: return np.zeros(n,float)
   # if n*M <= 200000 maybe direct to avoid FFT seg issues
   if n*M <= 200000:
      h=np.empty(n,float)
      for j in range(n):
         h[j]=float(np.dot(p, w[j:j+n]))
      return h
   L=_next_pow2(n+M-1)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   wr=w[::-1]
   fw=np.fft.rfft(wr,L)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,L)
   h=out[M-n:M][::-1].astype(float,copy=False)
   h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
   return h
```
Direct loop for n=512, M~1023 => n*M~524k >200k, uses FFT. For n=128, M=255, ~32k uses loop; loops Python 128 dots length128, okay but each dot vectorized. For many iterations, n=512 uses FFT. Good. Need ensure w[j:j+n] length maybe <n for j near end? In w length 2n-1, for j up to n-1, j+n-1 ≤2n-2, length n. Good. For direct, `np.dot(p, w[j:j+n])` fine.

Now, `_log_candidate`:
```
def _log_candidate(p,h,alpha):
    logp=np.log(p)  # p positive
    logq=logp - alpha*h
    mx=float(np.max(logq))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return None
    q=np.exp(logq-mx)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    return q/s
```
Need p positive; ensure. In MMD, p floor. For line search, h finite.

Place inside MMD? To avoid top-level? Can be top-level.

`_mmd_search`:
Let's write carefully.
```
def _mmd_search(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=80.0, beta_end=5000.0, tag='', verbose=False):
    p=_norm_seq(p0)
    if p is None:
       return None, float('inf')
    n=int(p.size)
    if time_limit <= 0 or n == 1:
       return p, _score_norm(p)
    floor=1e-15/float(n)
    p=np.maximum(p, floor); p /= p.sum()
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=_score_fast(p,n)
    cur=best_score
    beta=max(1.0, float(beta_start))
    beta_max=max(beta, float(beta_end))
    alpha=0.3
    stall=0
    it=0
    last_print=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_conv_self(p)
       c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
       M=float(np.max(c))
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: break
       w=_active_weights(c, beta)
       h=_grad_from_weights(w,p)
       h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       maxh=float(np.max(h))
       if maxh <= 1e-25:
          # perturb
          p_try=_log_perturb(p,rng,0.05)
          if p_try is not None:
             sc=_score_fast(p_try,n)
             if sc <= best_score*1.05:
                p=p_try; cur=sc
          stall += 1
       else:
          h=h/maxh
          # generate alphas
          factors=(0.125,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0)
          tests=[]
          for f in factors:
             a=alpha*f
             if a < 1e-5: a=1e-5
             if a > 80.0: a=80.0
             if len(tests)==0 or abs(a-tests[-1]) > 1e-12: # Need tests sorted? alpha*f increasing.
                tests.append(a)
          # maybe include 0.01 and 1.0
          extra=(0.02,0.1,1.0)
          for a in extra:
             if a>=1e-5 and a<=80.0:
                ok=True
                for b in tests:
                    if abs(a-b) <= 1e-10*(1+abs(a)):
                       ok=False; break
                if ok: tests.append(float(a))
          # sort
          tests.sort(key=float) # built-in? Could use tests.sort()
          logp_base=np.log(p)
          chosen=None; chosen_score=cur; chosen_alpha=alpha
          # Require strict improvement over current, not just best
          for a in tests:
             q=_log_candidate_from_log(logp_base,h,a)
             if q is None: continue
             sc=_score_fast(q,n)
             if sc < chosen_score - 1e-12:
                chosen=q; chosen_score=sc; chosen_alpha=a
          if chosen is not None:
             p=chosen; cur=chosen_score; alpha=chosen_alpha
             if cur < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
             stall=0
             # modest beta increase every few? outside
          else:
             alpha *= 0.6
             if alpha < 1e-5: alpha=1e-5
             stall += 1
             if stall == 15:
                beta = max(float(beta_start)*0.3, beta*0.7)
                p_try=_log_perturb(p,rng,0.05)
                if p_try is not None:
                   sc=_score_fast(p_try,n)
                   if sc <= best_score*1.08:
                      p=p_try; cur=sc
             if stall >= 50:
                p=best.copy(); cur=best_score; alpha=0.3; beta=beta_start*0.5; stall=0
       it +=1
       if it % 20 == 0:
          beta=min(beta_max, beta*1.04)
       if verbose and (it - last_print >= 200) and cur < float('inf'):
          last_print=it
          print(tag, 'it', it, 'cur', cur, 'best', best_score, flush=True)
    return best, best_score
```
Need `tests.sort()` no lambda. Good.

def _log_perturb(p, rng, std):
   logp=np.log(p)+rng.normal(0.0,std,p.size)
   mx=max(logp); q=exp(logp-mx); s=sum; return q/s
```

def _block_polish(p0, rng, time_limit):
   p=_norm_seq(p0)
   if p is None: return None, inf
   n=p.size; if n==1: return
   floor=1e-15/float(n); p=np.maximum(p,floor); p/=p.sum()
   best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(p,n); cur=best_score
   factors = np.exp(np.linspace(-2.0, 2.0, 11))
   deadline=...
   stale=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      r=float(rng.random())
      if r < 0.25:
          w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//1000 + 1)))
      elif r < 0.7:
          low=max(2, n//200); high=max(low+1, n//50 + 1)
          w=int(rng.integers(low, high+1))
      else:
          low=max(3, n//50); high=max(low+1, min(n//2, n//5 + 1))
          w=int(rng.integers(low, high+1))
      w=max(1,min(n-1,w))  # not whole
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      base=p
      found_current=base; found_score=cur
      for f in factors:
          q=base.copy()
          q[start:start+w] *= float(f)
          q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
          s=q.sum()
          if s <=0: continue
          q /= s
          sc=_score_fast(q,n)
          if sc < found_score - 1e-12:
             found=q; found_score=sc
      if found_current is not base:
          p=found_current; cur=found_score; stale=0
          if cur<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
      else:
          stale +=1
          if stale > 60:
             p_try=_log_perturb(p,rng,0.02)
             if p_try is not None:
                 sc=_score_fast(p_try,n)
                 if sc < cur*1.02:
                     p=p_try; cur=sc
             stale=0
          if stale > 300:
             p=best.copy(); cur=best_score; stale=0
   return best,best_score
```

def _lp_improve(p0, time_limit=60.0, lp_n=256, max_iters=4):
   if linprog is None: return None, inf
   p=_norm_seq(p0)
   if p is None: return None
   target_n=int(lp_n)
   if p.size != target_n:
       p=_resample(p, target_n)
   if p is None: return None
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-9/float(p.size)); p/=p.sum()
   best=p; best_score=_score_fast(p,p.size)
   start=time.monotonic()
   for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
       if time.monotonic() - start > time_limit*0.9: break
       g=_lp_g_from_p(p)
       if g is None: break
       # blend line search
       current_best=best? We should update p only if blend improves current p? Use p_work=best?
       cand_best=p.copy(); cand_score=best_score
       for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          q=(1.0-t)*p + t*g
          q=_norm_seq(q)
          if q is None: continue
          sc=_score_fast(q,q.size)
          if sc < cand_score - 1e-12:
              cand_best=q; cand_score=sc
       if cand_best is not p:
           p=cand_best; best_score=cand_score; best=p.copy()
       else:
           break
   return best, best_score
```

`_lp_g_from_p`:
Need if p size too large maybe downsample to 256 inside? `_lp_improve` did. In `_lp_g_from_p`, just solve.
```
def _lp_g_from_p(p):
   p=_norm_seq(p)
   if p is None: return None
   n=p.size
   if n > 512: p=_resample(p,512); n=p.size
   if n < 4: return None
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-8/float(n)); p/=p.sum()
   c=_conv_self(p); M=float(np.max(c))
   if M <=0 or not finite: return None
   B=np.zeros((2*n-1,n),float)
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k); cnt=hi-lo+1
      if cnt>0:
         B[k, lo:hi+1] = p[k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)]
   obj=-np.ones(n, float)
   b=np.full(2*n-1, M, float)
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
   res=None
   try:
      res=linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
   except Exception:
      try:
         res=linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='simplex')
      except Exception:
         res=None
   if res is None: return None
   x=getattr(res,'x',None)
   if x is None: return None
   g=np.asarray(x,float)
   g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   s=float(g.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300: return None
   return g/s
```
`method='simplex'` may be slow but only if highs fails. Could remove fallback. Keep.

Potential issue: For p floor, B rows may be tiny but constraints M ~ maybe 0.001; LP solution g sum can be huge? The objective max sum g. Since constraints limit g_i ≤ M / max_k p_{k-i}. If p floor 1e-8/n≈4e-11 for n256, M≈? maybe 0.001. Then g_i can be 25000, sum huge; but normalized g unaffected? The LP extreme might allocate huge mass to low p rows. Normalized shape maybe places g where p small? That could be good? It might be unbounded if some column all p values zero; with floor positive, bounded large but finite. Could numerical problems. Use smaller floor 1e-12/n to limit. But if p_i zeros, columns tiny, LP g huge maybe overflow. Use floor 1e-6/n? That distorts. Maybe `linprog` can handle. But variables might be 1e10 causing scaling issues. We can add an upper bound on g? In SOTA, they maximize sum g; scaling g to match normalization after. The optimum g scale is determined by constraints M and p values. If p has tiny floor, some constraints loose so g huge, but normalized shape may be valid (distributes mass where p small? Actually g_i unbounded if p column small; maximizing sum pushes to variables not in active constraints, i.e. where current p is small, so g becomes concentrated where p is small. This may be intended?). But huge values can cause LP numerical issues. We can impose variable bounds maybe (0, 1e6) after noting scale arbitrary? Since only normalized shape matters, we can add bounds 0 ≤ g_i ≤ Gmax without losing direction? If constraints permit much larger, optimum shape might be influenced by scale? Adding a cap could change shape if true g_i needs >Gmax. But since we normalize, a cap is like limiting individual mass relative to sum. Could use Gmax=1e6; p min floor 1e-9/n, M~0.001 => individual bound maybe 1e6, sufficient. Better: normalize M? Use p floor 1e-6/n? Hmm.

Maybe skip LP if any p_i < threshold? We can smooth p before LP: `p=np.maximum(p, 1e-6/float(n))`, this raises zeros to ~4e-9/256? 1e-6/256=3.9e-9. Then M/g bounds ~1e6? okay. Use floor 1e-7? The shape distortion small. We can use `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-7/float(n))`.

`_smooth_array`:
```
def _smooth_array(p, sigma):
   p=_norm_seq(p)
   if p is None: return None
   n=p.size
   if n<5 or sigma<=0: return p
   d=float(sigma)
   rad=int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0*d)))
   k=np.arange(-rad, rad+1, dtype=float)
   kern=np.exp(-0.5*(k/d)**2)
   ksum=kern.sum()
   if ksum<=0: return p
   kern/=ksum
   out=np.convolve(p, kern, mode='same')
   return _norm_seq(out)
```
For direct convolve p length n with kern length maybe small; if n large and sigma small, okay. But for n=4096 and K blocks, np.convolve direct n*rad maybe if rad=3 small; okay. Could use FFT if rad large; not needed.

`_random_start`:
```
def _random_start(n, rng, mode=None):
    if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,7))
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    if mode==0:
       z=np.zeros(n,float)
       Kmin=8; Kmax=14
       K=int(rng.integers(Kmin,Kmax+1))
       for k in range(1,K+1):
           amp=float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0/float(k+1)))
           phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
           z += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*t + phase)
           amp2=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.7/float(k+1)))
           z += amp2*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*k*t + phase*0.5)
       p=np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z,-40.0,40.0)))
    elif mode==1:
       a=float(rng.uniform(-1.2,4.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(-1.2,4.0))
       tt=np.clip(t,1e-6,1.0-1e-6)
       p=np.power(tt,a)*np.power(1.0-tt,b)
    elif mode==2:
       K=int(rng.integers(1,6)); p=np.zeros(n,float)
       centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K))
       sigmas=rng.uniform(0.015,0.30,K)
       weights=rng.uniform(0.3,2.0,K)
       for i in range(K):
          c=float(centers[i]); s=max(1e-4,float(sigmas[i])); w=float(weights[i])
          p += w*np.exp(-((t-c)/s)**2)
    elif mode==3:
       K=int(rng.integers(2, 16))
       edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,K-1))
       blocks=np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [1.0]))
       heights=abs(rng.normal(1.0,1.0,K))
       p=np.zeros(n,float)
       for i in range(K):
          s=round(blocks[i]*n); e=round(blocks[i+1]*n)
          e=max(e,s+1); p[s:e]=float(heights[i])
       p=_smooth_array(p, 1.5)
       if p is None: p=np.sin? fallback
    elif mode==4:
       z=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
       # random lowpass smoothing with FFT? simple moving average/cumsum? Use _smooth_array exp(z)
       sig=float(rng.uniform(2.0,20.0))
       p=np.exp(np.clip(z,-4,4))
       p=_smooth_array(p,sig)
    elif mode==5:
       # skewed rational
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); k=float(rng.uniform(3.0,15.0))
       p=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(k*(t-c),-50,50)))
       p=p * np.exp(-(((t-c)/0.25)**2))
    else:
       # mixture of edge beta and Fourier
       a=float(rng.uniform(0.2,2.0)); b=float(rng.uniform(0.2,2.0))
       t2=np.clip(t,1e-6,1-1e-6)
       p=np.power(t2, a)*np.power(1-t2,b)
       z=0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + rng.uniform(0,6))*float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0))
       p = p * np.exp(z)
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0) + 1e-12
    return _norm_seq(p)
```
If mode3 smoothing returns None? It shouldn't. But if K-1=0? K≥2. Good.
Use `np.concatenate` allowed.

def _noise_vec(a, rng, std):

`_arrays_close`:
```
def _arrays_close(a,b,eps=0.1):
   if a.size != b.size: return False
   d=float(np.linalg.norm(a-b))
   return d < eps
```

Now, time stage in propose. Let's write pseudo with careful rem.
```
def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 60: budget=60.0
    deadline=t0+max(60.0,budget-30.0)
    try: iseed=int(seed)
    except: iseed=12345
    rng=np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007)+1)
    cands=[]
    parent_arr=None
    try:
       parent_raw=height_sequence_1
       parent_arr=_norm_seq(parent_raw)
    except Exception:
       parent_arr=None
    if parent_arr is not None:
       _add_candidate(cands,parent_arr,_score_norm(parent_arr),30)
       print('parent score', ... , parent_arr.size)
    # decide final_n
    if deadline - t0 > 1600: final_n=4096
    elif deadline - t0 > 900: final_n=3072
    else: final_n=2048
    # coarse
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 350:
       coarse_n = final_n//2 if final_n>=1024 else 256
       coarse_n = max(256, min(512, coarse_n))
       coarse_starts=[]
       if parent_arr is not None:
          coarse_starts.append(_resample(parent_arr, coarse_n))
       for mode in (0,2,3,1,4,5):
          coarse_starts.append(_random_start(coarse_n, rng, mode))
       coarse_scored=[]
       for a in coarse_starts:
          if a is None: continue
          sc=_score_norm(a)
          if np.isfinite(sc): coarse_scored.append((sc,a))
       coarse_scored.sort(key=_item_score)
       for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(coarse_scored[:6]):
          rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
          if rem < 40: break
          talloc=min(35.0, max(8.0, rem/8.0))
          p,psc=_mmd_search(a,rng,talloc,beta_start=40.0,beta_end=800.0,tag='coarse')
          _add_candidate(cands,p,psc,30)
          print('coarse', idx, psc)
       # LP on best coarse
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem > 80 and linprog is not None:
          # find best candidate size <= coarse_n? Could just use best from cands not parent? choose best score with size==coarse_n maybe
          best_coarse=None
          for item in sorted(cands,key=_item_score):
              if item[1] == coarse_n:
                  best_coarse=item[2]; break
          if best_coarse is None and coarse_scored:
              best_coarse=coarse_scored[0][1]
          if best_coarse is not None:
              lp_p, lp_sc = _lp_improve(best_coarse, time_limit=min(60.0, rem*0.4), lp_n=coarse_n if coarse_n<=256 else 256, max_iters=3)
              _add_candidate(cands, lp_p, lp_sc, 30)
              if lp_p is not None: print('lp', lp_sc)
```

Now, let's refine `_mmd_search` code details:
```
def _mmd_search(...):
    p = _norm_seq(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float('inf')
    n = int(p.size)
    if time_limit <= 0 or n <= 1:
        return p, _score_norm(p)
    floor = 1e-15 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p /= p.sum()
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p, n)
    cur_score = best_score
    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_start))
    beta_max = max(beta, float(beta_end))
    alpha = 0.3
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -10000
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p /= p.sum()
        c = _conv_self(p)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        if c.size == 0: break
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300: break
        w = _active_weights(c, beta)
        h = _grad_from_weights(w, p)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(h))
        if maxh <= 1e-25:
            p_try = _log_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
            if p_try is not None:
                sc = _score_fast(p_try, n)
                if sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                    p = p_try
                    cur_score = sc
            stall += 1
        else:
            h = h / maxh
            factors = (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0)
            tests = []
            for f in factors:
                a = float(alpha) * float(f)
                if a < 1e-5: a = 1e-5
                if a > 80.0: a = 80.0
                tests.append(a)
            for a in (0.02, 0.1, 1.0):
                tests.append(float(a))
            # sort and unique-ish
            tests.sort()
            # remove duplicates
            if tests:
                uniq=[tests[0]]
                for a in tests[1:]:
                    if abs(a - uniq[-1]) > 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(a)):
                        uniq.append(a)
                tests=uniq
            logp_base = np.log(p)
            chosen = None
            chosen_score = cur_score
            chosen_alpha = alpha
            for a in tests:
                q = _log_candidate_from_log(logp_base, h, a)
                if q is None: continue
                sc = _score_fast(q, n)
                if sc < chosen_score - 1e-12:
                    chosen = q
                    chosen_score = sc
                    chosen_alpha = a
            if chosen is not None:
                p = chosen
                cur_score = chosen_score
                alpha = chosen_alpha
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                stall = 0
            else:
                alpha *= 0.6
                if alpha < 1e-5: alpha=1e-5
                stall += 1
                if stall == 15:
                    beta = max(float(beta_start)*0.3, beta*0.7)
                    p_try = _log_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
                    if p_try is not None:
                        sc = _score_fast(p_try, n)
                        if sc <= best_score * 1.08:
                            p = p_try
                            cur_score = sc
                if stall >= 50:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    alpha = 0.3
                    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_start)*0.5)
                    stall = 0
        it += 1
        if it % 20 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.04)
        if verbose and (it - last_print >= 200):
            last_print = it
            print(tag, 'it', it, 'cur', repr(cur_score), 'best', repr(best_score), flush=True)
    return best, float(best_score)
```

def _log_candidate_from_log(logp, h, alpha):
   logq = logp - alpha*h
   mx=float(np.max(logq))
   if not np.isfinite(mx): return None
   q=np.exp(logq - mx)
   s=float(q.sum())
   if s<=1e-300 or not finite: return None
   return q/s
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(arr, m):
    a=_norm_seq(arr)
    if a is None: return None
    if int(m) <=0: return None
    if a.size == m: return a.copy()
    n=a.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, float)
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/float(m)
    out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0) + 1e-12
    return _norm_seq(out)
```
np.interp expects xp increasing; yes. If n=1, returns array of ones. Good.

```
def _noise_vec(a, rng, std):
   a=_norm_seq(a)
   if a is None: return None
   if std <= 0: return a
   q=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, a.size))
   return _norm_seq(q)
```

def _arrays_close(a,b,eps=0.12):

```
def _log_perturb(p, rng, std):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size ==0: return None
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-30)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    p=p/s
    logp=np.log(p) + rng.normal(0.0, std, p.size)
    mx=float(np.max(logp))
    if not finite: return None
    q=np.exp(logp - mx)
    s=float(q.sum())
    if s <=0 or not finite: return None
    return q/s
```
Good.

def _constant_starts(n, count=4):
   starts=[]
   if n<=0: return starts
   starts.append(np.ones(n, float)/float(n))
   t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)

      starts.append(p/p.sum())

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)**2

by combining:
  * multi-scale multiplicative active-set gradient descent on the simplex,
  * random Fourier / block / beta / bump starts,
  * optional LP polishing inspired by active-constraint updates,
  * exact coordinate/block-scaling local search,
  * optional up-polish to larger length.

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, which does not change the score.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _item_score(item):
    return float(item[0])


def _item_score_pair(item):
    return float(item[0])


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _norm_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return arr / s


def _conv_self(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([p[0] * p[0]], dtype=float)
    if n * n <= 2000000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(p, p), dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)
    return out[:2 * n - 1].astype(float, copy=False)


def _score_fast(p, n):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.size != n:
        n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        p = p / s
    c = _conv_self(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _score_norm(a):
    p = _norm_seq(a)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    return _score_fast(p, n)


def _active_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    n = int(c.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    z = c / m - 1.0
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    e = np.exp(beta * z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    return e / s


def _grad_from_weights(w, p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if m < n:
        w = np.concatenate((w, np.zeros(n - m, dtype=float)))
        m = n
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    # h[j] = sum_i p[i] w[j+i]
    if n * m <= 200000:
        h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        for j in range(n):
            seg = w[j:j + n]
            if seg.size < n:
                seg = np.concatenate((seg, np.zeros(n - seg.size, dtype=float)))
            elif seg.size > n:
                seg = seg[:n]
            h[j] = float(np.dot(p, seg))
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        return h

    L = _next_pow2(n + m - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, L)
    h = out[m - n:m][::-1].astype(float, copy=False)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def _log_candidate_from_log(logp, h, alpha):
    logq = logp - float(alpha) * h
    mx = float(np.max(logq))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return None
    q = np.exp(logq - mx)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    q = q / s
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return q / s


def _log_perturb(p, rng, std):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-30)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = p / s
    logp = np.log(p) + rng.normal(0.0, float(std), p.size)
    logp = np.where(np.isfinite(logp), logp, -700.0)
    mx = float(np.max(logp))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return None
    q = np.exp(logp - mx)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    q = q / s
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    return q


def _resample(arr, target):
    t = int(target)
    if t <= 0:
        return None
    a = _norm_seq(arr)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a.size == t:
        return a.copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(t, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(t, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(t)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    out = np.maximum(np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0), 0.0) + 1e-12
    return _norm_seq(out)


def _noise_vec(a, rng, std):
    if a is None:
        return None
    arr = _norm_seq(a)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    if std <= 0.0:
        return arr
    q = arr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(std), arr.size))
    return _norm_seq(q)


def _arrays_close(a, b, eps=0.12):
    if a.size != b.size:
        return False
    d = float(np.linalg.norm(a - b))
    return d < float(eps)


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, score, keep=32):
    a = _norm_seq(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score_norm(a)
    cands.append((float(score), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    if len(cands) > keep:
        cands.sort(key=_item_score)
        cands[:] = cands[:keep]


def _smooth_array(p, sigma):
    p = _norm_seq(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 5 or sigma <= 0.0:
        return p
    sig = float(sigma)
    rad = int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0 * sig)))
    k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1, dtype=float)
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / sig) ** 2)
    s = float(kern.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return p
    kern = kern / s
    out = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    return _norm_seq(out)


def _constant_starts(n, count=4):
    starts = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return starts
    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    for a in (0.4, 0.8, 1.2, 1.6):
        p = np.maximum(1.0 + a * (2.0 * t - 1.0), 1e-8)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            starts.append(p / s)
        p = np.maximum(1.0 - a * (2.0 * t - 1.0), 1e-8)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            starts.append(p / s)
    return starts[:max(1, int(count))]


def _random_start(n, rng, mode=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if mode is None:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 7))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if mode == 0:
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        K = int(rng.integers(8, 15))
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp1 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / float(k + 1)))
            amp2 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7 / float(k + 1)))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += amp1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + phase)
            z += amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + 0.5 * phase)
        p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)))

    elif mode == 1:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 4.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 4.0))
        tt = np.clip(t, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        p = np.power(tt, a) * np.power(1.0 - tt, b)

    elif mode == 2:
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        sigmas = rng.uniform(0.015, 0.30, K)
        weights = rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0, K)
        for i in range(K):
            c = float(centers[i])
            s = max(1e-4, float(sigmas[i]))
            w = float(weights[i])
            p = p + w * np.exp(-((t - c) / s) ** 2)

    elif mode == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 18))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1))
        blocks = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0], dtype=float), edges, np.array([1.0], dtype=float)))
        heights = np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, 1.0, K)) + 0.05
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for i in range(K):
            s = int(round(blocks[i] * n))
            e = int(round(blocks[i + 1] * n))
            s = min(n, max(0, s))
            e = min(n, max(0, e))
            if e > s:
                p[s:e] = float(heights[i])
        p = _smooth_array(p, 1.5)
        if p is None:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    elif mode == 4:
        z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0))
        p = _smooth_array(p, float(rng.uniform(2.0, 25.0)))
        if p is None:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    elif mode == 5:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        k = float(rng.uniform(3.0, 15.0))
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(k * (t - c), -50.0, 50.0)))
        p = p * np.exp(-((t - c) / 0.25) ** 2)

    else:
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5))
        tt = np.clip(t, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        base = np.power(tt, a) * np.power(1.0 - tt, b)
        z = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28)))
        p = base * np.exp(np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0))

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0) + 1e-12
    return _norm_seq(p)


def _mmd_search(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=80.0, beta_end=5000.0, tag="", verbose=False):
    p = _norm_seq(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n <= 1:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    floor = 1e-15 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p, n)
    cur_score = best_score

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_start))
    beta_max = max(beta, float(beta_end))
    alpha = 0.3
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -10000
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            break
        p = p / s

        c = _conv_self(p)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
            break

        w = _active_weights(c, beta)
        h = _grad_from_weights(w, p)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(h))

        if maxh <= 1e-25:
            p_try = _log_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
            if p_try is not None:
                sc = _score_fast(p_try, n)
                if sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                    p = p_try
                    cur_score = sc
            stall += 1
        else:
            h = h / maxh

            factors = (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0)
            tests = []
            for f in factors:
                a = float(alpha) * float(f)
                if a < 1e-5:
                    a = 1e-5
                if a > 80.0:
                    a = 80.0
                tests.append(a)
            for a in (0.02, 0.1, 1.0):
                tests.append(float(a))
            tests.sort()
            if tests:
                uniq = [tests[0]]
                for a in tests[1:]:
                    if abs(a - uniq[-1]) > 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(a)):
                        uniq.append(a)
                tests = uniq

            logp_base = np.log(p)
            chosen = None
            chosen_score = cur_score
            chosen_alpha = alpha

            for a in tests:
                q = _log_candidate_from_log(logp_base, h, a)
                if q is None:
                    continue
                sc = _score_fast(q, n)
                if sc < chosen_score - 1e-12:
                    chosen = q
                    chosen_score = sc
                    chosen_alpha = a

            if chosen is not None:
                p = chosen
                cur_score = chosen_score
                alpha = chosen_alpha
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                stall = 0
            else:
                alpha *= 0.6
                if alpha < 1e-5:
                    alpha = 1e-5
                stall += 1

                if stall == 15:
                    beta = max(float(beta_start) * 0.3, beta * 0.7)
                    p_try = _log_perturb(p, rng, 0.05)
                    if p_try is not None:
                        sc = _score_fast(p_try, n)
                        if sc <= best_score * 1.08:
                            p = p_try
                            cur_score = sc

                if stall >= 50:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    alpha = 0.3
                    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_start) * 0.5)
                    stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 20 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.04)
        if verbose and (it - last_print >= 200):
            last_print = it
            print(tag, "it", it, "cur", repr(cur_score), "best", repr(best_score), flush=True)

    return best, float(best_score)


def _block_polish(p0, rng, time_limit):
    p = _norm_seq(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    floor = 1e-15 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p, n)
    cur_score = best_score

    factors = np.exp(np.linspace(-2.5, 2.5, 13, dtype=float))
    stale = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.25:
            w = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 1000 + 1)))
        elif r < 0.70:
            low = max(2, n // 200)
            high = max(low + 1, n // 50 + 1)
            w = int(rng.integers(low, high + 1))
        else:
            low = max(3, n // 50)
            high = max(low + 1, min(n // 2, n // 5 + 1))
            w = int(rng.integers(low, high + 1))

        w = int(max(1, min(n - 1, w)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        found = None
        found_score = cur_score

        for f in factors:
            q = p.copy()
            q[start:start + w] *= float(f)
            q = np.maximum(np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0), 0.0)
            s = float(q.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            q = q / s
            sc = _score_fast(q, n)
            if sc < found_score - 1e-12:
                found = q
                found_score = sc

        if found is not None:
            p = found
            cur_score = found_score
            stale = 0
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 60:
                p_try = _log_perturb(p, rng, 0.02)
                if p_try is not None:
                    sc = _score_fast(p_try, n)
                    if sc < cur_score * 1.02:
                        p = p_try
                        cur_score = sc
                stale = 0
            if stale > 300:
                p = best.copy()
                cur_score = best_score
                stale = 0

    return best, float(best_score)


def _lp_g_from_p(p):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    p = _norm_seq(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = int(p.size)
    if n > 512:
        p = _resample(p, 512)
        if p is None:
            return None
        n = int(p.size)
    if n < 4:
        return None

    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9 / float(n))
    p = p / p.sum()

    c = _conv_self(p)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    B = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=float)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
            B[k, lo:hi + 1] = p[k - idx]

    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=float)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1e8)] * n

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub=B, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="simplex")
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        return None
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_improve(p0, time_limit=60.0, lp_n=256, max_iters=4):
    if linprog is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    p = _norm_seq(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    target = int(lp_n)
    if p.size != target:
        q = _resample(p, target)
        if q is None:
            return p, _score_norm(p)
        p = q

    if p.size < 8:
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9 / float(p.size))
        p = p / p.sum()
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9 / float(p.size))
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p, int(p.size))
    start = time.monotonic()

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - start > float(time_limit) * 0.9:
            break
        g = _lp_g_from_p(p)
        if g is None or g.size != p.size:
            break

        cand_best = p
        cand_score = best_score
        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            q = (1.0 - float(t)) * p + float(t) * g
            q = _norm_seq(q)
            if q is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_fast(q, int(q.size))
            if sc < cand_score - 1e-12:
                cand_best = q
                cand_score = sc

        if cand_best is not p:
            p = cand_best
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = cand_score
        else:
            break

    return best, float(best_score)


def _alloc_time(deadline, desired):
    rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
    if rem <= 20.0:
        return 0.0
    return max(0.0, min(float(desired), rem - 20.0))


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation function.
    Returns a plain Python list of floats, normalized to sum 1.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        budget = 60.0

    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 30.0)

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007) + 1)

    cands = []

    # Optional previous best.
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
        parent_arr = _norm_seq(parent_raw)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        if parent_arr.size > 16384:
            pr = _resample(parent_arr, 8192)
            if pr is not None:
                parent_arr = pr
        _add_candidate(cands, parent_arr, _score_norm(parent_arr), 32)
        print("parent score:", _item_score((parent_arr.size, _score_norm(parent_arr))),
              "len", int(parent_arr.size), flush=True)

    rem0 = deadline - t0
    if rem0 > 1600.0:
        final_n = 4096
    elif rem0 > 900.0:
        final_n = 3072
    else:
        final_n = 2048

    # ----------------------- Coarse exploration -----------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 350.0:
        coarse_n = max(256, min(512, final_n // 2 if final_n >= 1024 else 256))
        coarse_starts = []

        if parent_arr is not None:
            coarse_starts.append(_resample(parent_arr, coarse_n))

        coarse_starts.extend(_constant_starts(coarse_n, 3))
        for mode in (0, 2, 3, 1, 4, 5):
            coarse_starts.append(_random_start(coarse_n, rng, mode))

        coarse_scored = []
        for a in coarse_starts:
            if a is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_norm(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                coarse_scored.append((sc, a))
        coarse_scored.sort(key=_item_score)

        for idx, item in enumerate(coarse_scored[:6]):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 40.0:
                break
            talloc = _alloc_time(deadline, min(35.0, max(8.0, rem / 8.0)))
            if talloc <= 5.0:
                break
            p, sc = _mmd_search(item[1], rng, talloc,
                                beta_start=40.0, beta_end=800.0,
                                tag="coarse{} ".format(idx), verbose=False)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 32)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                print("coarse", idx, "score", repr(float(sc)), flush=True)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 80.0 and linprog is not None:
            best_coarse = None
            for item in sorted(cands, key=_item_score):
                if item[1] == coarse_n and item[2] is not None:
                    best_coarse = item[2]
                    break
            if best_coarse is None and coarse_scored:
                best_coarse = coarse_scored[0][1]
            if best_coarse is not None:
                talloc = _alloc_time(deadline, min(70.0, 0.35 * rem))
                if talloc > 10.0:
                    lp_p, lp_sc = _lp_improve(best_coarse, time_limit=talloc,
                                              lp_n=min(256, coarse_n), max_iters=3)
                    if lp_p is not None:
                        _add_candidate(cands, lp_p, lp_sc, 32)
                        print("lp coarse score:", repr(lp_sc) if np.isfinite(lp_sc) else "inf", flush=True)

    # ----------------------- Build final-length starts -----------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    start_sources = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        start_sources.append(_noise_vec(_resample(parent_arr, final_n), rng, 0.02))

    top_cands = sorted(cands, key=_item_score)[:3]
    for item in top_cands:
        if item[2] is None:
            continue
        start_sources.append(_noise_vec(_resample(item[2], final_n), rng, 0.03))

    start_sources.extend(_constant_starts(final_n, 3))
    for mode in (0, 2, 3, 1, 4):
        start_sources.append(_random_start(final_n, rng, mode))

    scored_starts = []
    for a in start_sources:
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_starts.append((sc, a))
    scored_starts.sort(key=_item_score)

    final_starts = []
    for sc, a in scored_starts:
        if len(final_starts) >= 4:
            break
        dup = False
        for b in final_starts:
            if _arrays_close(a, b, 0.12):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            final_starts.append(a)

    if not final_starts:
        a = _random_start(final_n, rng, 0)
        if a is None:
            a = np.ones(final_n, dtype=float)
        final_starts = [a]

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # ----------------------- Main multi-start polishing -----------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0:
        reserve = 0.35 * rem
        reserve = max(300.0, min(900.0, reserve))
        main_budget = max(60.0, rem - reserve)
        fracs = (0.50, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10)

        for idx, a in enumerate(final_starts):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 100.0:
                break
            frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.05
            talloc = float(main_budget) * float(frac)
            talloc = min(talloc, max(30.0, rem - max(90.0, reserve * 0.2)))
            talloc = _alloc_time(deadline, talloc)
            if talloc <= 20.0:
                break

            p, sc = _mmd_search(a, rng, talloc,
                                beta_start=80.0, beta_end=8000.0,
                                tag="main{} ".format(idx), verbose=False)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 32)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_arr = p.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                print("main", idx, "best", repr(best_score), "len", int(p.size), flush=True)

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if best_score < 1.506 and rem > 800.0:
                break

    if best_arr is None:
        for item in sorted(cands, key=_item_score):
            if item[2] is not None:
                best_arr = item[2].copy()
                best_score = float(item[0])
                break
        if best_arr is None:
            best_arr = final_starts[0].copy()
            best_score = _score_norm(best_arr)

    if best_arr.size != final_n:
        up = _resample(best_arr, final_n)
        if up is not None:
            sc = _score_norm(up)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if best_score > 1.55 or int(up.size) > int(best_arr.size):
                    best_arr = up
                    best_score = float(sc)

    # ----------------------- Optional large-length lift -----------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500.0 and best_arr is not None and best_arr.size < 8192:
        n_big = int(min(8192, max(2 * int(best_arr.size), 2 * final_n)))
        big = _resample(best_arr, n_big)
        if big is not None:
            t1 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(300.0, 0.35 * rem))
            if t1 > 20.0:
                pb, scb = _mmd_search(big, rng, t1,
                                      beta_start=300.0, beta_end=10000.0,
                                      tag="big1 ", verbose=False)
                if pb is not None and np.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_score:
                    best_arr = pb.copy()
                    best_score = float(scb)
                    print("big1 best", repr(best_score), "len", int(pb.size), flush=True)
                _add_candidate(cands, pb, scb, 32)

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 120.0:
                t2 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(120.0, 0.25 * rem))
                if t2 > 30.0:
                    use = best_arr if best_arr.size == n_big else (big if big is not None else best_arr)
                    pl, scl = _block_polish(use, rng, t2)
                    if pl is not None and np.isfinite(scl):
                        _add_candidate(cands, pl, scl, 32)
                        if scl < best_score:
                            best_arr = pl.copy()
                            best_score = float(scl)
                            print("big2 block best", repr(best_score), "len", int(pl.size), flush=True)

    # ----------------------- Final polishing at chosen length -----------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t1 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(600.0, 0.35 * rem))
        if t1 > 20.0:
            p, sc = _mmd_search(best_arr, rng, t1,
                                beta_start=250.0, beta_end=15000.0,
                                tag="pol1 ", verbose=False)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 32)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_arr = p.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                print("pol1 best", repr(best_score), "len", int(p.size), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t2 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(500.0, 0.35 * rem))
        if t2 > 20.0:
            p, sc = _block_polish(best_arr, rng, t2)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 32)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_arr = p.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                print("pol2 block best", repr(best_score), "len", int(p.size), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t3 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(400.0, 0.5 * rem))
        if t3 > 20.0:
            p, sc = _mmd_search(best_arr, rng, t3,
                                beta_start=800.0, beta_end=30000.0,
                                tag="pol3 ", verbose=False)
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 32)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_arr = p.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
                print("pol3 best", repr(best_score), "len", int(p.size), flush=True)

    # One last low-resolution LP nudge, if time and scipy are available.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0 and best_arr is not None and linprog is not None:
        t4 = _alloc_time(deadline, min(35.0, 0.35 * rem))
        if t4 > 10.0:
            lp_p, lp_sc = _lp_improve(best_arr, time_limit=t4, lp_n=256, max_iters=2)
            if lp_p is not None:
                up = _resample(lp_p, int(best_arr.size))
                if up is not None:
                    sc = _score_norm(up)
                    _add_candidate(cands, up, sc, 32)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_arr = up
                        best_score = float(sc)

    # ----------------------- Final official checks -----------------------
    eval_list = []
    if best_arr is not None:
        eval_list.append((best_score, best_arr))

    for item in sorted(cands, key=_item_score):
        if len(eval_list) >= 8:
            break
        if item[2] is not None and item[1] <= 12000:
            eval_list.append((float(item[0]), item[2]))

    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size <= 12000:
        eval_list.append((_score_norm(parent_arr), parent_arr))

    eval_list.sort(key=_item_score_pair)

    selected_list = [float(x) for x in (best_arr if best_arr is not None else np.ones(final_n, dtype=float))]
    selected_score = best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")

    evaluated = 0
    for sc, arr in eval_list:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 6.0:
            break
        if evaluated >= 4:
            break
        a = _norm_seq(arr)
        if a is None:
            continue
        if a.size > 10000 and evaluated >= 1:
            continue
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            official = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            official = float(official)
        except Exception:
            official = _score_norm(a)
        if np.isfinite(official) and official < selected_score:
            selected_score = official
            selected_list = lst
        evaluated += 1

    out = np.asarray(selected_list, dtype=float).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(max(1, final_n), dtype=float)
        s = float(out.size)
    out = out / s

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
